- **lesefassung-konsolidiert**: Konsolidierte Lesefassung des geaenderten Stammgesetzes nach Inkrafttreten erstellen. Anwendungsfall Fachreferat Vollzugsbehoerde oder Anwalt will wissen wie das Gesetz nach Aenderung aussieht ohne Aenderungsmarkierungen. Einheitlich lesbar ohne Aenderungsmarkierung Datierung Stand nach Inkrafttreten. Format-Konventionen Inkrafttretens-Handling Verweis-Pflege DOCX-Ausgabe. Output Lesefassung pro Stammgesetz bereinigt datiert. Abgrenzung zu synopse-erstellen Gegenüberestellung alt und neu. ([Koftakhor/germanlegal](https://github.com/Koftakhor/germanlegal/tree/HEAD/legistik-werkstatt/skills/lesefassung-konsolidiert) · ★1)

- **lesejahr-rueckblick**: Erstellt einen persönlichen Rückblick auf ein Lesejahr aus Eselsohr, mit Anzahl Bücher, Seiten, Lieblingen, Formaten und Genres. Verwenden bei Fragen wie „Wie war mein Lesejahr?“, „Wie viele Bücher habe ich 2026 gelesen?“ oder „Welche Genres habe ich dieses Jahr gelesen?“. ([subthiel-eu/eselsohr-claude-plugin](https://github.com/subthiel-eu/eselsohr-claude-plugin/tree/HEAD/skills/lesejahr-rueckblick))
- **lesen**: Wertet eine Quelle eines Wikis im OKF-Format aus und prüft das Ergebnis gegen (/lesen &lt;slug&gt;). Prüft zuerst die Nutzungsrechte; nur wenn die KI-Auswertung erlaubt ist, entwirft das Modell der Rolle lesen Kurzreferat und Kernaussagen mit Fundstellen und wörtlichen Ankern. Claude prüft jeden Anker am Original, schreibt die Karte, dann prüft ein frischer Prüfer (Rolle gegenpruefen) jede Aussage gegen das Original. Nutzen, wenn eine Karte Inhalt bekommen soll, vor einem Produkt aus dem Wiki (/lesen --pruefen &lt;seite&gt;…) und als Abschluss von /goal-Läufen. ([sina-769/okfw](https://github.com/sina-769/okfw/tree/HEAD/skills/lesen) · ★1)

- **Leslie Lamport**: Leslie Lamport transformed how we reason about distributed systems. He invented logical clocks and the concept of happened-before relationships, enabling causal reasoning across distributed processes. His Paxos algorithm solved the consensus problem with surprising elegance. TLA+ (Temporal Logic of Actions) brought formal specification to industry-scale systems. LaTeX, the de facto standard for sc ([yfyang86/turingskill](https://github.com/yfyang86/turingskill/tree/HEAD/leslie-lamport))
- **Leslie Lamport**: Leslie Lamport 改变了我们推理分布式系统的方式。他发明了逻辑时钟和"发生在前"关系，使跨分布式进程的因果推理成为可能。他的 Paxos 算法以惊人的优雅解决了共识问题。TLA+（动作时序逻辑）将形式规范带到了工业规模系统。LaTeX，作为科学排版的事实标准，源于他的实际需求。Lamport 将数学严谨性与冷面幽默相结合，坚持认为正确的规范比聪明的实现更重要。 ([yfyang86/turingskill](https://github.com/yfyang86/turingskill/tree/HEAD/turingmind-cn/leslie-lamport))
- **Leslie Valiant**: Leslie Valiant is a theoretical computer scientist who created the mathematical foundations of machine learning and parallel computing. His PAC (Probably Approximately Correct) learning model provided the first rigorous framework for understanding when and how machines can learn from data. He revolutionized complexity theory with #P-completeness, showing the computational difficulty of counting pr ([yfyang86/turingskill](https://github.com/yfyang86/turingskill/tree/HEAD/leslie-valiant))
- **Leslie Valiant**: Leslie Valiant 是一位理论计算机科学家，创建了机器学习和平行计算数学基础。他的 PAC（可能近似正确）学习模型为理解机器何时以及如何从数据中学习提供了首个严谨框架。他用 #P 完全性彻底改变了复杂性理论，表明计数问题的计算难度。他的 BSP 模型实现了实用的并行算法设计。自 1982 年加入哈佛大学以来，Valiant 为以前由启发式主导的领域带来了形式严谨性，从神经网络到进化生物学。 ([yfyang86/turingskill](https://github.com/yfyang86/turingskill/tree/HEAD/turingmind-cn/leslie-valiant))
- **leslie-hardware-test**: Protocol for verifying editor behavior on the real Leslie 2101/2103 mk2 with front-panel dumps, byte-level diffs and fixtures. Use when a feature depends on how the machine reacts (a new parameter, a new message type, memory writes), when a byte of the dump is still unknown, or when the user reports what the Leslie or the editor showed during a test. ([jeanfrancoisperrault/leslie-mk2-editor](https://github.com/jeanfrancoisperrault/leslie-mk2-editor/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/leslie-hardware-test))
- **leslie-midi-safety**: Safety rules for any code or action that sends MIDI to the Leslie 2101/2103 mk2 (NRPN, CC, SysEx, dumps). Use before adding or changing anything in src/midi, src/sysex, the store's send path, Save to memory, or when preparing a .syx file or a MIDI message for the user to send to the machine. ([jeanfrancoisperrault/leslie-mk2-editor](https://github.com/jeanfrancoisperrault/leslie-mk2-editor/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/leslie-midi-safety))

- **less**: Re-renders the previous response shorter: one sentence, two, or a paragraph. Use for tldr, condense, too long, brevity cap. ([gpu-cli/skills](https://github.com/gpu-cli/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/less) · ★1)
- **less**: Make code smaller without changing what it does - cut what isn't load-bearing, use what the standard library, the platform, or an installed dependency already does, collapse abstractions with one caller - then list what was removed and why, and what was kept because it's the code. User-invoked with /silicon-valley:less. ([harshitt13/Silicon-Valley-Skill](https://github.com/harshitt13/Silicon-Valley-Skill/tree/HEAD/plugins/silicon-valley/skills/less))
- **less**: Less — CSS preprocessor with variables, nesting, mixins, and functions that compiles to plain CSS on Node.js and the browser. ([hieudoanm/hieudoanm.github.io](https://github.com/hieudoanm/hieudoanm.github.io/tree/HEAD/.skills/programming/languages/css/preprocessor/less))
- **less-aggressive-attack**: Tests for vulnerabilities less aggressively — read-only where possible, confirming a flaw without exercising its full impact or causing damage, under a strict set of safety rules. Not the default; activate only when the user explicitly signals safety as a constraint, e.g. "don't break anything", "read-only", "non-destructive testing", "careful with production", "keep it clean", or "just prove the finding, don't exploit it fully". ([Rifteo/skills](https://github.com/Rifteo/skills/tree/HEAD/less-aggressive-attack) · ★38 · 2 copies)
- **less-ai-tone**: Use when finishing Chinese prose drafts (chapters, reports, essays, novel revisions) and need to detect or remove AI-writing traces before delivery; also when another writing skill reaches a long-form deliverable and must run a post-draft cleanup gate. ([iannil/skills](https://github.com/iannil/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/book-content-audit/less-ai-tone) · ★3 · 31 copies)
- **less-ai-tone**: 按 SKILL.md 明确列出的规则识别并改写写作中的 AI 痕迹。只能处理清单内的问题；未命中规则的文字必须原样保留，也不能改变文章框架。适用于写作完成后的成稿清理。 Remove AI writing tells using an explicit whitelist of rules; leaves unmatched text untouched. ([Kumoi-S/myStack](https://github.com/Kumoi-S/myStack/tree/HEAD/sources/etc/less-ai-tone))
- **less-ai-tone-en**: Remove AI tone from finished English prose in three layers. A script grades candidates (A mechanical / B suggested / C report-only / H human review / X do-not-touch / F fingerprint), the model labels four semantic checks (elegant variation, cohesion chains, conclusion colons, isomorphic triples), and the user first gets a readable pre-edit report with counts, evidence strength, quoted sentences and concrete fixes.
